Motivation: 经验风险最小化训练的模型在存在虚假相关性的数据上平均准确率高，但在相关性不成立的子群体上性能差。现有方法需要早期训练信号并依赖群体标注验证集来选择干预 epoch，作者希望无需群体标注且不依赖早停就能检测受虚假相关影响的样本。

Method: 在模型收敛后，对每个输入施加固定扰动（如对抗扰动），统计预测标签是否翻转。与虚假相关一致的样本分类规则鲁棒，不易翻转；而不一致的样本拟合更脆弱，易被扰动翻转。通过两次前向传播即可获得检测信号，无需群体标注或早停。

Result: 在 Waterbirds 数据集上，利用检测到的样本重新平衡训练，最差群体准确率从 57.3% 提升至 80.8%，而使用真实群体标签的 baseline 为 85.8%。

Conclusion: 该方法证明收敛后的模型仍包含可用于识别虚假相关样本的信号（通过扰动敏感性），为无群体标注的鲁棒学习提供了简单有效的途径。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [292] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息流网络（IFNs），将生成流网络（AFlowNets）扩展到不完全信息博弈中，证明原有约束不适用于获得有效策略密度和训练目标，并实验表明IFNs在性能与速度上优于或匹敌OSMCCFR和标准RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法多混合强化学习与反事实后悔方法以权衡计算速度与性能，但对不完全信息博弈中的生成采样框架研究较少。作者希望将生成流网络从完全信息博弈推广到不完全信息博弈，并解决原有约束在此场景下失效的问题。

Method: 提出信息流网络（IFNs），作为对抗流网络（AFlowNets）在不完全信息博弈中的推广；理论证明原有生成流网络的约束在不完全信息博弈中无法得到有效策略密度和训练目标，IFNs通过新的泛化形式解决该问题，并严格包含AFlowNets作为特例。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境中，IFNs在性能与速度上与基于Outcome Sampling蒙特卡洛反事实后悔（OSMCCFR）的方法及标准RL方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: IFNs有效将生成流网络扩展到不完全信息博弈，为博弈论中的生成式采样框架提供了新的理论支撑和实用方法。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [293] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出生成训练中的“第三预训练轴”并非探索（Explorative Modeling, XM）本身，而是“自由度”（freedom）——即模型行为约束的弱化程度。理论证明XM的损失取决于候选输出错过可接受区域的概率，探索将该概率提升至K次幂，且当K>1时匹配概率随自由度增加。实验表明：更大的K值提高或饱和了测量到的自由度；在30个案例中，基于自由度的选择器在29个中胜过验证集选择。因此，XM是手段，自由度是目的，选择自由度能改善分布偏移下的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将探索（Explorative Modeling, XM）视为生成表达性的“第三预训练轴”，但本文旨在揭示这一轴的本质实际上是“自由度”（freedom）——即行为约束的弱化程度，而非探索本身。作者希望从理论和实验上阐明自由度才是泛化能力的关键，并证明XM通过提高自由度来生效。

Method: 理论分析：证明平均XM损失取决于候选输出错过可接受区域的概率，探索将该概率提升至K次幂，并推导出当K>1时匹配概率随自由度上升。实验一：Forward XM实验，改变K值测量自由度，在上下文相关目标下验证K增加对自由度的提升作用。实验二：训练XM候选池，将基于未标注父上下文选择候选的自由度选择器与验证集选择器进行对比（30个案例）。

Result: 理论证明：对于K>1，匹配概率（模型输出落入可接受区域的可能性）随自由度增加而上升。Forward XM实验中，更大的K值提高或饱和了测量到的自由度，在上下文相关目标下每个测试值均增加了自由度。候选池比较中，自由度选择器在30个案例中29次胜出。此外，生成表达性仅是模式计数的代理，丢失了自由度赋予泛化意义的扩展结构。

Conclusion: 探索性建模（XM）是实现自由度的手段，而自由度才是真正的目标。第三预训练轴并非探索，而是自由度。选择自由度可以改进XM在分布偏移下的表现，且自由度是比生成表达性更本质的泛化属性。

Abstract: In generative training, a model produces an output and is penalised for its difference from an example. With one output per comparison, a model that produces one common answer can outperform a model retaining a broader repertoire. Explorative Modeling (XM) produces $K$ outputs per comparison and updates on the closest, claiming exploration as a "third pretraining axis" associated with generative expressivity. Here I show the third axis is actually freedom, meaning the weakness of the constraint implied by a model's behaviour. Previous work showed freedom is a property of function rather than form. Parameters, architecture, minimum-description-length (MDL), and data can vary while the behavioural constraint remains unchanged. It was formally proved that weakest models are likeliest to generalise, and freedom selection beat MDL by 110-500\% in induction experiments. I prove average XM loss depends on the chance a candidate misses an acceptable region, with exploration raising miss probability to power $K$. For $K>1$, match probability rises with freedom. I then demonstrate empirically that XM optimises for freedom. In a Forward XM experiment, larger $K$ increased or saturated measured freedom, and increased freedom at every tested value under context-dependent targets. I trained XM candidate pools and compared validation selection with a freedom selector that read unlabelled parent contexts. Freedom won in 29 of 30 cases. Generative expressivity is a mode-count proxy for freedom, that discards the extension structure that gives freedom its generalisation significance. XM is a means, freedom an end, and selecting for freedom improved XM under distribution shift.

</details>


### [294] [EvoHarness-RL: Learning Self-Evolving Runtime Harness for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05446)
*Xuying Ning,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Hanqing Zeng,Yuanchen Bei,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Li,Qifan Wang,Xiang Shen,Yifan Wu,Jiayi Liu,Hong Li,Yinglong Xia,Xiangjun Fan,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EvoHarness-RL框架，通过离线学习可训练的"马甲"策略（BPE状态+监督微调+成本感知GRPO），让LLM智能体在长程任务中自主构建和协调外部工作区状态，在ALFWorld上达到96.9%成功率，并揭示了"马甲退火"和"马甲进化"两种关键动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体越来越依赖外部执行支持来维护状态、追踪进度、调用工具、验证结果等，但有效的马甲使用面临两个耦合挑战：从带噪声的交互轨迹中形成状态，以及对访问外部状态的运行时控制。现有方法依赖提示、启发式或领域特定约定，外部工作区及其使用策略需要人工设计，缺乏可学习的自动化策略。

Method: 提出EvoHarness-RL，将信念（Belief）、进度（Progress）和经验（Experience）暴露为面向策略的马甲状态。先通过监督马甲微调教会基础智能体马甲动作空间及如何构建有用外部状态，再使用成本感知GRPO探索协调策略，在长程交互中选择性读取、更新和整合状态。在ALFWorld上使用Qwen3-8B LLM实例化。

Result: EvoHarness-RL在ALFWorld上达到96.9%成功率。揭示了两种关键动态：马甲退火——训练将重复的马甲使用模式内化到模型策略中，使智能体从频繁调用马甲转向选择性访问外部状态；马甲进化——进度更新和经验整合将马甲优化为紧凑、任务自适应的状态基板。

Conclusion: 长程智能体能从可训练的策略中获益，用于构建和协调外部马甲工作区，这比简单地增加更强的工具或更大的记忆更有价值。马甲策略学习有望替代人工设计的外部工作区使用规则。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents increasingly rely on external execution support to maintain state, track progress, invoke tools, verify outcomes, and reuse experience across interactions. However, effective harness use raises two coupled challenges: state formation from noisy interaction traces and runtime control over external-state access. Existing agents usually handle both through prompts, heuristics, or domain-specific conventions, leaving the external workspace and its usage policy manually engineered. To address this, we study the problem of harness policy learning, where agents learn harness policies offline and deploy them to construct and update external harness state online during runtime task execution. We introduce EvoHarness-RL, which exposes Belief, Progress, and Experience (BPE) as policy-facing harness state. Supervised harness fine-tuning teaches the base agent the harness action space and how to construct useful external state, while cost-aware GRPO explores coordination policies to selectively read, update, and consolidate that state during long-horizon interaction. Instantiated on ALFWorld with a Qwen3-8B LLM, EvoHarness-RL reaches 96.9% success and reveals two key dynamics: harness annealing, where training internalizes recurring harness-use patterns into the model policy and shifts the agent from frequent harness calls toward selective external-state access, and harness evolution, where progress updates and experience consolidation refine the harness into a compact, task-adaptive state substrate. These results suggest that long-horizon agents benefit from trainable policies for constructing and coordinating with external harness workspaces, beyond simply adding stronger tools or larger memories.

</details>


### [295] [Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotopes for Identifiable and Refinable Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05454)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出混合概率Zonotope（HProbZ）作为神经网络的新型概率预测头，将不确定性分解为三种可识别来源：离散模态选择（二值生成器）、有界系统漂移（有界生成器）和不可约随机噪声（随机生成器），通过共享有界生成器实现步骤间代数耦合，支持闭式似然、分析性风险及多模态保形集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测头（高斯混合或单保形区域）无法分离预测任务中三种不同的不确定性来源：离散模态选择、模态内有界系统漂移和不可约随机噪声。这限制了模型对复杂不确定性的表达能力和可解释性。

Method: HProbZ将三种不确定性编码为zonotope的三个生成器（二值、有界、随机），通过卷积实现闭式似然。共享有界生成器使各预测步骤代数耦合，单次前向传播即可利用观测步骤精化剩余步骤的预测分布。理论证明了三个生成器在似然下可辨识（仅置换意义），且HProbZ密度与任何有限高斯混合在表示上不同。

Result: 在代表性预测基准上的实证分析表明，与相同编码器的混合基线相比，HProbZ在有效性上具有优势，同时提供了混合或凸保形预测器无法共同具备的结构性质（分析性每模态风险、分布自由的多模态保形集合）。

Conclusion: HProbZ通过显式分离并建模三种不确定性来源，提供了比高斯混合和单保形区域更丰富的预测表征能力，兼具可解释性、闭式推理和保形校准特性，为概率预测头设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction heads in neural networks typically output either a Gaussian mixture or a single conformal region. Neither separates the distinct sources of uncertainty often present in real prediction tasks: a discrete choice among modes, bounded systematic drift within the chosen mode, and irreducible stochastic noise. We introduce the Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ), an output head that represents these three sources as binary, bounded, and stochastic generators of a zonotope, and admits a closed-form likelihood by convolution. Sharing the bounded generator across prediction steps couples future predictions algebraically, so observing one step refines the predictive distribution at every remaining step in a single forward pass. We establish that the three generators are identifiable from the likelihood up to permutation, and that an HProbZ density is representationally distinct from any finite Gaussian mixture. The same shared structure provides analytic per-mode risk and distribution-free multi-modal conformal sets at inference time. Empirical analysis on representative prediction benchmarks supports the effectiveness of the design relative to same-encoder mixture baselines, while offering structural properties that mixture or convex-conformal predictors do not jointly provide.

</details>


### [296] [Matrix Zonotopic Attention: A Context-Adaptive Value Projection for Set Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05472)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出标准多头注意力（MHA）中值投影与输入无关，导致对排列不变集目标的表示能力受限。通过引入变换自由度（TDOF）度量目标复杂度，证明刚性值投影的注意力需要深度与TDOF成正比，而上下文自适应的值族单层即可表示。据此提出矩阵带状注意力（MZAttn），用输入依赖的门控矩阵带状族替代固定值投影，在初始化时退化为标准MHA，保持排列等变性。实验表明该架构在高度依赖输入集合的高秩稀疏组合式目标上优势明显，而对聚合统计类目标优势不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多头注意力的值投影是输入无关的线性变换，而路由是输入相关的，这种不对称性限制了模型对排列不变集目标的表达能力。作者希望理解这种不对称性带来的表示局限，并设计一种既能保持标准MHA高效初始化优势、又能提升表达能力的改进方案。

Method: 1) 提出变换自由度（TDOF）作为目标算子的复杂度度量，计数精确表示所需的输入相关方向数。2) 进行深度分离分析：证明上下文刚性（固定值投影）注意力需要深度与TDOF成正比才能表示目标，而上下文自适应值族单层即可。3) 基于此提出矩阵带状注意力（MZAttn）：将固定值投影替换为上下文自适应的矩阵带状族——一个中心矩阵加上由输入依赖门控加权的生成矩阵之和。该构造在初始化时退化到标准MHA，保持排列等变性，并具有数据驱动可达性解释。

Result: 在多个集合预测任务上的实验与TDOF预测一致：架构优势具有选择性——在目标依赖于输入集合的高秩、稀疏组合方式时明显，而在聚合统计类目标（标准注意力已足够）上优势较小。

Conclusion: 标准MHA的刚性值投影是深度表示的关键瓶颈；通过上下文自适应值族（MZAttn）可在单层内达到更大表示能力，同时保留标准初始化和排列等变性。这为改进Transformer架构提供了理论指导，并揭示了注意力机制中输入依赖路由与输入独立投影之间的不对称性本质。

Abstract: Multi-head attention combines an input-dependent softmax routing with an input-independent linear value projection, so the per-sample operator mapping aggregated values to outputs is the same for every input set. We study the consequences of this asymmetry for permutation-invariant set targets. We introduce the Transformation Degrees of Freedom (TDOF) of a target operator, a complexity measure counting the input-dependent directions an exact representation requires, and present a depth-separation analysis showing that context-rigid attention needs depth proportional to the target's TDOF, whereas a single layer with a context-adaptive value family can represent the same target. Building on this analysis, we propose Matrix Zonotopic Attention (MZAttn), which replaces the fixed value projection with a context-adaptive matrix-zonotope family: a centre matrix plus a sum of generator matrices weighted by input-dependent gates. The construction reduces to standard multi-head attention at initialisation, preserves permutation equivariance, and admits a data-driven reachability interpretation. Experiments on a range of set-prediction tasks are consistent with the TDOF prediction that the architectural advantage is selective: it appears on targets that depend on the input set in a high-rank, sparsely combinatorial way, and is small on aggregate-statistic targets where parameter-matched standard attention is already competitive.

</details>


### [297] [KV-Skill: Forging Expertise in the Model's Native Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05475)
*Zhaowei Han,Xiang Zhang,Bing Han,Kai Liu,Danqi Hu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KV-Skill 提出一种外部因子化算子设计空间，将任务知识压缩为轻量级接口（KV-Skill），冻结的骨干模型通过该接口读取知识。支持两条路径：注册（将文本技能转化为文本派生算子并训练共享接口）和奖励学习（直接从任务结果学习紧凑潜在算子）。在多个基准和骨干模型上，该方法显著优于文本技能、SoftSkill、Soft Prefix、Prefix Tuning 和 LoRA 等方法，且支持独立加载和共享接口无遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见的任务知识存储方式（文本提示或权重更新）各有局限：文本需每次解释，权重适应性导致难以独立加载、移除或共享。本文旨在设计一种外部、可组合、与骨干模型分离的知识表示。

Method: 提出 KV-Skill 设计空间，基于外部因子化算子（KV-Skill），冻结的语言模型通过轻量级接口读取。包含两条互补路径：1) 注册（Registration）：将手写文本技能转换为文本派生算子，并训练每骨干的共享接口；2) 奖励学习（Reward Learning）：直接从任务结果学习紧凑潜在算子（可有或没有文本技能）。两条路径均不增加提示中的位置 token。

Result: 在十个基准和四个骨干（三个模型家族）上，将文本转为 KV-Skill 一致提升有效性。在 Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath 上，注册达到 77.2 准确率，远超源文本技能 23.4、SkillOpt 52.0 和 SoftSkill 64.5。在匹配奖励训练和参数预算下，KV-Skill 在八个设置中七个取得最佳结果（对比 Soft Prefix、Prefix Tuning、LoRA）。事后秩分析表明，文本派生算子每个注入层仅需一个任务对齐方向即可保留几乎全部收益，随机方向则失败。一个共享接口可加载三个独立 KV-Skill 而无遗忘。

Conclusion: 任务知识可以从文本或经验中获取，压缩为外部算子，并与骨干模型分离部署。该方法实现了模块化、可组合、可独立加载的知识表示，且性能优越。

Abstract: Task knowledge is commonly stored either as text in the prompt or as an update to model weights. Text is modular but must be interpreted on every use, while weight adaptation makes the resulting capability difficult to load, remove, or share independently. We introduce KV-Skill, a design space of external factorized operators that a frozen language model reads through a lightweight interface. KV-Skill supports two complementary paths. Registration converts an authored text skill into a text-derived operator and trains a shared per-backbone interface. Reward learning develops a compact latent operator directly from task outcomes, with or without an authored skill. Neither path adds positions to the prompt. Across ten benchmarks and four backbones from three model families, converting text to a KV-Skill consistently makes the same procedural knowledge more effective. On Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath, registration reaches 77.2 accuracy, compared with 23.4 for the source text skill, 52.0 for SkillOpt, and 64.5 for SoftSkill. Under matched reward training and parameter budgets, KV-Skill gives the best result in seven of eight matched settings against soft prefixes, prefix tuning, and LoRA. A post-hoc rank analysis further shows that text-derived operators retain nearly all of their benefit with one task-aligned direction per injection layer, while matched random directions fail. Finally, one shared interface retains three independently loadable KV-Skills without measurable forgetting. These results show that task knowledge can be acquired from text or experience, compressed into an external operator, and deployed separately from the backbone. Code is available at: https://github.com/shawnzhg/KV-Skill

</details>


### [298] [Align-RAG: Alignment Is All You Need for TSFM In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05571)
*Mohammad Asadi,Soheil Hor,Bardiya Akhbari,Jack W. O'Sullivan,Tahoura Nedaee,Layne C. Price,Raviteja Anantha,Euan Ashley,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Align-RAG 是一种无需训练的检索增强时间序列预测方法，通过对检索到的过去-未来窗口进行闭式幅度缩放和整数滞后相位对齐，使冻结的基础模型（TSFM）能够动态利用检索上下文，在多个数据集上优于需要训练的融合适配器，平均 MSE 降低 3.75%，并进一步在四种不同架构的冻结 TSFM 上提升零样本性能 2.5%–13.7%。研究表明，冻结模型本身已支持上下文学习，闭式对齐应作为检索增强预测的默认基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强时间序列预测方法通常依赖训练过的融合模块（适配器）来合并检索示例，假设冻结的基础模型无法动态融入检索上下文。作者试图证明这一假设并非必要，探索无需训练的对齐方法。

Method: 提出 Align-RAG，一种无需训练的方法，对检索到的过去-未来窗口对应用闭式幅度缩放和整数滞后相位偏移（即振幅重缩放与相位对齐），然后将其作为上下文输入冻结的基础模型（TSFM），无需任何可学习参数。

Result: 在 Chronos-Bolt 上，Align-RAG 在所有七个标准数据集上均优于训练过的检索适配器，平均 MSE 降低 3.75%；在额外四种不同架构的冻结 TSFM 上，零样本 MSE 提升 2.5%–13.7%，且无需针对每个 backbone 调参。对齐后的演示诱导的预测偏移与闭式岭回归预测器的偏移一致，说明冻结模型能动态进行上下文学习。

Conclusion: 冻结的时间序列基础模型已经具备动态上下文学习能力，闭式对齐应作为检索增强时间序列预测的默认基线，在训练任何融合模块之前使用。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented forecasting promises to adapt frozen Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) to new domains without fine-tuning, but recent methods typically rely on learned fusion modules, i.e., trained adapters that merge retrieved examples into the backbone's forecast, based on the assumption that frozen backbones cannot dynamically incorporate retrieved context on their own. We show this assumption is unnecessary. We introduce Align-RAG, a training-free method that applies a closed-form per-pair amplitude rescaling and integer-lag phase shift to retrieved past-future windows before they enter a frozen backbone's context. With no learned parameters, Align-RAG outperforms the state-of-the-art trained retrieval adapter on a frozen Chronos-Bolt on all seven datasets of the standard benchmark (avg -3.75% MSE), showing that the gains previously attributed to learned fusion are recoverable without any training. Align-RAG further improves zero-shot MSE on four additional frozen TSFMs with various architectures by 2.5% to 13.7% per backbone with no per-backbone tuning. To probe why alignment helps, we compare the frozen backbone's prediction shift under aligned demonstrations to the closed-form ridge prediction shift on the same pairs. We find that aligned demonstrations induce prediction shifts that track a closed-form ridge predictor on the same pairs, with a future-shuffle control ruling out a futures-averaging account. Together, these results indicate that frozen TSFMs already support dynamic in-context use of retrievals, and that closed-form alignment should be the default baseline for retrieval-augmented forecasting before any fusion module is trained. Code available at: https://github.com/masadi-99/align-rag

</details>


### [299] [LC-GRPO: Bridging Train-Inference Gap for Flow-Based GRPO with Langevin Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05600)
*Yingqing Guo,Hui Yuan,Zijian He,Mengdi Wang,Zheng Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LC-GRPO，一种带有朗之万校正的流模型GRPO框架。在每个 rollout 步骤中，先执行与推理一致的ODE欧拉步，再应用随机朗之万校正以匹配边际分布。该方法消除了随机训练 rollout 与确定性测试 ODE 推理之间的分布不匹配，在文本到图像和文本到视频任务上一致提升了奖励优化效果并保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习需要随机 rollout 进行策略探索，但流模型通常用确定性ODE采样；现有方法用SDE替代ODE进行训练，但有限步离散化后ODE与SDE产生显著差异：SDE rollout 随探索噪声增大变得模糊，与测试时ODE采样器生成的样本不匹配，影响强化学习效果。

Method: LC-GRPO：每个 rollout 步骤先执行一个与推理对齐的ODE欧拉步，再施加一个随机朗之万校正步骤，目标是当前时间步的边际分布。所需分数直接从流速度中获得（无需额外分数模型），得到的转移核是各向同性高斯分布，具有可处理的似然用于策略优化。理论上证明在一个理想条件下，一步朗之万校正能降低不完美ODE欧拉步的Wasserstein误差；在同等随机性水平下，所提转移比反向SDE的标准Euler–Maruyama离散化更精确。

Result: 在SD3.5-Medium、FLUX.1-Dev和HunyuanVideo上的实验表明，LC-GRPO在文本到图像和文本到视频任务上一致改善奖励优化，保持生成质量，并显著缩小了随机训练 rollout 与确定性测试 ODE 推理之间的差距。

Conclusion: LC-GRPO通过朗之万校正有效弥合了流模型中随机训练 rollout 与确定性测试 ODE 推理之间的差异，在保持生成质量的同时提升了强化学习奖励优化效果，为流模型在在线强化学习中的应用提供了更一致的训练-推理对齐方案。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models are typically sampled by solving a deterministic ordinary differential equation (ODE), whereas online reinforcement learning requires stochastic rollouts for policy exploration and optimization. Existing GRPO methods for flow models therefore replace the inference-time ODE with a stochastic differential equation (SDE) during training. Although the ODE and SDE share the same marginal distributions in continuous time, their finite-step discretizations can differ substantially. In particular, SDE rollouts often become blurry as the exploration noise increases, creating a mismatch between the samples used for reinforcement learning and those generated by the test-time ODE sampler. We introduce LC-GRPO, a flow-based GRPO framework with Langevin correction. Each rollout transition first takes an inference-aligned ODE Euler step and then applies a stochastic Langevin correction targeting the marginal distribution at the resulting timestep. The required score is recovered directly from the flow velocity, requiring no additional score model, while the resulting transition remains an isotropic Gaussian with a tractable likelihood for policy optimization. We theoretically show that, under suitable conditions, one Langevin correction step reduces the Wasserstein error of an imperfect ODE Euler step. At a matched randomness level, we further show that the proposed transition can be more accurate than the standard Euler--Maruyama discretization of the reverse SDE. Experiments on SD3.5-Medium, FLUX.1-Dev, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate that LC-GRPO consistently improves reward optimization across text-to-image and text-to-video tasks, preserves generation quality, and substantially narrows the gap between stochastic training rollouts and deterministic test-time ODE inference.

</details>


### [300] [GAUGE: Granularity-Adaptive Counterfactual Gating of Evidence for Incomplete Multimodal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05608)
*Yunping Shi,En Yu,Kairui Guo,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAUGE是一个轻量级的反事实门控框架，用于处理不完整多模态分类问题。它通过冻结的填补器补全缺失模态，将观测和恢复的模态统一编码为细粒度证据单元，利用泰勒证据分数在单次前向-反向传播中高效估计每个单元的反事实效应，并映射为连续门控以产生注意力logit偏置，实现单元级别证据调制而不改变骨干架构。在六个基准上超越强基线，泰勒余项理论分析验证了方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在粗粒度模态层面处理不完整多模态输入，无法在同一个恢复模态中保留可靠成分同时抑制误导成分，损害预测可靠性。需要一种细粒度的证据控制方法来区分可靠与不可靠的证据单元。

Method: 提出GAUGE框架：1) 使用冻结的填补器对缺失模态进行填补；2) 将观测和恢复的模态统一编码为细粒度证据单元；3) 通过预测感知的泰勒证据分数在单次前向-反向传播中计算每个单元替换为参考表征的反事实效应；4) 将分数映射为连续门控，转换为加性注意力logit偏置，实现单元级别证据调制而不改变骨干架构。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，GAUGE在各种不完整输入设置下均优于强基线方法。泰勒余项理论分析表明一阶近似相对于精确反事实效应的误差可控，证明了方法的原理性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: GAUGE是一种原理性且可扩展的细粒度证据控制框架，通过反事实门控和泰勒近似有效处理模态不完整性问题，在保持轻量级的同时显著提升分类可靠性。

Abstract: Multimodal classification typically assumes all modalities are available, yet real-world inputs are often incomplete. Imputation and dynamic fusion can mitigate such incompleteness, but existing methods operate at a coarse modality level and thus cannot retain reliable components while suppressing misleading ones within the same recovered modality, compromising prediction reliability. To address this issue, we propose GAUGE, a lightweight counterfactual gating framework for incomplete multimodal classification. GAUGE first imputes missing modalities with a frozen imputer and encodes observed and recovered inputs uniformly as fine-grained evidence units. Rather than intervening on each unit explicitly, GAUGE scores the counterfactual effect of replacing every unit with a reference representation through prediction-aware Taylor evidence scores, all obtained in a single forward-backward pass. These scores are mapped to continuous gates, which are converted into additive attention-logit biases for unit-wise evidence modulation without altering the backbone architecture. Experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate that GAUGE outperforms strong baselines across diverse incomplete-input settings. Furthermore, a Taylor remainder theoretical analysis characterizes the error of the first-order approximation relative to the exact counterfactual effect, establishing GAUGE as a principled and scalable framework for fine-grained evidence control under modality incompleteness.

</details>


### [301] [Reasoning Errors Have a Region and a Direction in the Residual-Stream Trajectory of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05660)
*Hamed Damirchi,Ignacio Meza De la Jara,Damith Ranasinghe,Yuhang Liu,Javen Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种三流探测器（motion + region + direction），通过状态条件化的运动（state-conditioned motion）而非静态状态或去上下文轨迹来更准确地判断语言模型推理的正确性，在推理基准上相比位移-only方法提升12%准确率，并泛化至事实性任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于轨迹的方法通过层间残差流位移（displacement）来检测推理正确性，但位移忽略了更新前的原始状态（location），而恢复完整状态又会引入捷径相关信息。作者识别出这一权衡，希望设计一种方法能够保留足够的状态上下文来解读运动，同时避免返回完整状态探测。

Method: 提出三流探测器：（1）motion流：残差位移；（2）region流：基于向量量化的粗粒度区域读取器，捕捉状态所在的粗略区域；（3）direction流：基于归一化多层状态的细粒度方向读取器。三者结合，在保留状态上下文的同时避免完整状态探测的缺陷。

Result: 在未见过的推理基准上，选择准确率比位移-only最先进方法提升12%，比单层探测基线提升21%。仅用推理基准训练，即可在事实补全和事实验证任务上超越所有对照检测器。消融实验证明三个流提供互补信号。

Conclusion: 推理有效性应当从状态条件化的运动（state-conditioned motion）中读取，而非仅从静态状态或去上下文的轨迹中读取。三流探测器实现了对推理正确性更可靠的判别，并泛化至非推理任务，表明信号捕捉的是正确性本身而非特定推理模式。

Abstract: As language models are increasingly used for tasks that require verifiable reasoning, reliably distinguishing sound reasoning from flawed reasoning has become an important practical problem. Recent trajectory-based methods seek this signal in layerwise residual-stream displacements, which capture how representations change while attenuating some stable, token-specific information. However, displacement omits the state from which an update originates, whereas restoring the full state risks reintroducing shortcut-prone information. We identify this trade-off and propose a three-stream detector that combines motion with two restricted views of location. A coarse region reader based on vector quantization and a fine direction reader over normalized multi-layer states. This design restores enough state context to interpret the motion without returning to full-state probing. On reasoning benchmarks unseen during training, our method improves selection accuracy by up to 12% over the displacement-only state of the art and 21% over single-layer probing baselines. Although trained only on reasoning benchmarks, it also reads factual completion and fact verification, ahead of every detector we compare against, which places the signal on correctness rather than on a kind of reasoning. Ablations further show that motion, region, and direction provide complementary signals. These results suggest that reasoning validity is better read from state-conditioned motion than from either static states or decontextualized trajectories alone.

</details>


### [302] [Potential Matching Optimal Transport: Continuous Normalizing Flows for Exact $p$-Wasserstein Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05666)
*Lishuo Zhang,Ruizhi Huang,Yang Yu,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出势匹配最优传输（PMOT）框架，用于一般 p 代价的最优传输问题，通过标量势参数化 CNF 速度场，利用自诱导匹配损失沿直线桥梁训练，并证明了零损失下的精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优传输方法多针对特定代价（如平方 L2），缺乏用于一般 p 代价的灵活框架；PMOT 旨在统一处理 p-代价最优传输，同时保持终端分布匹配的灵活性。

Method: PMOT 将 CNF（连续归一化流）的速度场参数化为标量势（广义 Benamou-Brenier 形式），训练时通过模型自身端点确定的直线桥梁构建自诱导匹配损失，允许灵活的终端分布匹配。

Result: 主要结果建立了零损失精确性：在正则性、精确终端匹配和唯一性假设下，零损失解满足广义 Benamou-Brenier 最优性系统并恢复 p-最优传输映射。合成基准上学习到与 p-匹配 OT 参考一致的映射；在高维表格数据上作为似然密度模型表现竞争力，MMD 色彩变换实验展示了灵活的样本终端匹配。

Conclusion: PMOT 为一般 p-代价最优传输提供了有效的势流框架，兼具理论保证和实际灵活性，在密度建模和分布匹配任务中表现出色。

Abstract: We introduce Potential Matching Optimal Transport (PMOT), a potential-flow framework for general $p$-cost optimal transport with $c_p(x,y)=\|x-y\|^p$. PMOT parameterizes the CNF velocity field with a scalar potential in the generalized Benamou--Brenier form for the chosen exponent $p$. It trains the potential gradient with a self-induced matching loss along straight bridges determined by the model's own endpoints, while allowing flexible terminal distribution matching. Our main result establishes zero-loss exactness: under the stated regularity, exact terminal matching, and uniqueness assumptions, any zero-loss solution satisfies the generalized Benamou--Brenier optimality system and recovers the corresponding $p$-optimal transport map and dynamics. On synthetic benchmarks, PMOT learns $p$-specific maps that agree with the corresponding $p$-matched OT references. It also remains competitive as a likelihood-based density model on high-dimensional tabular data, and an MMD-based color transformation experiment demonstrates flexible sample-based terminal matching.

</details>


### [303] [When Does Consensus Mean Correctness? Measuring the Agreement-Accuracy Coupling with Semantics-Preserving Re-Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05670)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过程序化重绘科学图表（RENDEQ数据集）来研究视觉语言模型（VLM）的跨扰动一致性（agreement）与正确性之间的关系。发现重新渲染（re-rendering）优于重采样（resampling），一致性信号在多数模型上优于平均token对数概率基线，但基于自身一致性的微调反而导致准确率下降，揭示了一致性作为可靠性信号的条件和局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像扰动常隐含地假设语义不变性，但缺乏精确答案键来定位错误。科学图表通过程序绘制，重绘可产生语义等价且答案可精确对比的图像，为直接测量模型一致性（agreement）与正确性之间的耦合提供了理想测试平台。

Method: 构建RENDEQ数据集，基于科学图表程序化生成渲染等价（render-equivalence）的图像集合。在三个开源VLM上测量重新渲染与重采样策略的准确率和可靠性，比较一致性信号与平均token对数概率基线，并分析影响一致性的风格因素（如图表库）。进行基于模型自身跨渲染共识的微调实验，验证一致性作为训练目标的效果。

Result: 重新渲染在准确率和可靠性上均优于重采样。一致性信号在两个模型上优于平均token对数概率基线，在第三个模型上持平（纠正了之前管道bug导致的错误结果）。不一致性主要集中于一个风格因素——绘图库，其影响是次大因素的2倍以上，比噪声水平高一个数量级。基于自身跨渲染共识的微调导致所有五次复现运行中准确率均下降，与自然图像上的已发表结果符号相反。

Conclusion: 一致性仅在模型错误足够分散的阈值以上才能认证正确性；而直接优化一致性（作为训练目标）会破坏这种分散性，导致准确率下降。这揭示了一致性信号作为可靠性指标的条件性，并警示了将一致性用作自训练目标可能带来的负面影响。

Abstract: A model's agreement across perturbed inputs is used both as a label-free reliability signal and as a self-training target, on the premise that agreement tracks correctness. That coupling is rarely measured directly: natural-image perturbations preserve meaning only by assumption, and no exact answer key localizes errors. Scientific figures remove both obstacles, a figure is drawn from data by a program, so redrawing it yields images that are semantically equivalent by construction and share a programmatically exact answer. We build RENDEQ, a generator of such render-equivalence sets, and measure the coupling on three open-weight VLMs, checking every finding across three independent instantiations. Re-rendering beats resampling on both accuracy and reliability. Agreement beats an evidence-carrying baseline, mean token log-probability, on two of three models and ties on the third, reversing an intermediate, buggy replication traced to a rendering-pipeline failure. The dispersion behind this is concentrated in one style factor, the plotting library, more than double the next-largest factor and an order of magnitude above the noise floor. Fine-tuning on the model's own cross-render consensus inverts: accuracy falls in every one of five replication runs, the opposite sign to published results on natural images. Agreement certifies correctness only above a threshold set by how diffuse a model's errors are, and an objective that rewards agreement destroys exactly that diffuseness.

</details>


### [304] [Consistency Has a Computable Blind Spot: A Commutation Theory of Label-Free Reliability for Vision-Language Figure Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05675)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉-语言模型的无标签可靠性问题，指出基于不变性的方法存在盲点：系统性的误读可能通过不变性测试而被错误认证。作者提出利用等变性（equivariance）来补充不变性，即对输入数据进行编辑后，正确答案应按可计算量变化。他们证明了匹配的等变性编辑对于仿射读取错误是完备的，并提出了等变性-一致性分数（Equivariance-Consistency Score）作为无标签、无需训练的检测器。实验表明，该方法的预测排序在多个模型上成立，并揭示了不变性盲点的本质属于关系本身而非实现。同时，该理论解释了分类器变质测试文献中排序反转的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于不变性的无标签可靠性方法存在盲点：某些系统性的误读在输入扰动后保持不变，从而被错误地认证为正确。作者希望找到一种方法来检测这类错误，并揭示不变性盲点的计算本质。

Method: 作者从代数角度分析，指出错误对编辑不可见当且仅当两者可交换，因此无法被一组编辑检测到的错误构成该编辑集的联合中心化子。他们转而利用等变性：编辑数据后正确答案必须按可计算量变化。他们提出了匹配的等变性编辑（如仿射变换）并证明其对仿射读取错误完备；对于标签置换，交换编辑不完备而循环重标签可弥补大部分差距。据此设计了等变性-一致性分数（Equivariance-Consistency Score）作为检测器，并发布了REND-EQUIV数据集。

Result: 预测的检测能力排序在三个视觉-语言模型和手工标注群体上成立；另一种不变性方法确认盲点属于关系本身而非具体实现；循环重标签在真实样本上带来了预测的提升。该理论还解释了分类器变质测试文献中检测能力排序反转的现象。

Conclusion: 不变性和等变性是互补的可靠性检测关系；检测能力是关系与错误类的联合属性，而非仅由关系决定。等变性方法能填补不变性方法的盲点，为无标签可靠性评估提供了新的理论框架和实用工具。

Abstract: Label-free reliability for vision-language models rests on invariance: perturb the input and a faithful reader's answer should not change. This has a known blind spot, a systematic misreading survives the perturbation and gets certified wrong, which we show is computable, not just real: an error is invisible to an edit exactly when the two commute, so the errors a suite cannot reach form its joint centralizer, a set that shrinks as edits are added and can be written down rather than guessed at. We act on the complementary relation, equivariance: edit a figure's data and the correct answer must change by a computable amount. Two matched edits are provably complete for affine reading errors; no suite of swap edits is complete for label permutations, and cyclic relabeling closes most of that gap. We instantiate the theory as the Equivariance-Consistency Score, a label-free, training-free detector, and release REND-EQUIV, pairing matched invariance and equivariance sets over identical data. The predicted ordering holds across three models and a hand-labeled population immune to the one circularity in how it is selected; a second invariance-family method confirms the blind spot belongs to the relation, not to any implementation; and cyclic relabeling delivers its predicted gain on a matched real sample. The same characterization explains a reported inversion of this ordering in the classifier metamorphic-testing literature: detectability is a joint property of the relation and the fault class, never of the relation alone.

</details>


### [305] [SEAM: Global consistency beyond local accuracy in scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05702)
*Gnankan Landry Regis N'guessan,Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出科学解释适配性机器（SEAM），一个与生成器无关的框架，用于检验局部解释能否整合为全局一致的科学校验。SEAM-Ω将每个区域用结构化解释（状态、闭包、观测通道及可选的合同元数据）表示，比较相邻解释的重叠区域，将不一致转化为通道分辨的障碍，从而实现精确可修复性检验、残差感知归因，并分离不一致性与不可辨识性。在19个实验中，SEAM能检测局部预测准确但全局解释不兼容的情况，并将失败归因到特定通道和重叠区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学机器学习方法仅能在局部层面（子域、基准划分或单次预测解释）验证模型，无法判断这些局部解释能否拼接成全局一致的科学解释。作者试图解决这一局部到全局的一致性检验问题。

Method: SEAM框架采用有限解释层（explanation-sheaf）实例化SEAM-Ω，为每个区域构建包含状态、闭包和观测通道的结构化解释，并引入可选的合同元数据作为约束；通过比较相邻区域解释的重叠部分检测不一致，将不一致转化为通道分辨的障碍（obstruction）；利用精确可行性检验来反驳或保留候选解释账户，当精确修复不可行时采用残差感知正则化记录提供经验归因；同时分离不一致性与不可辨识性，并监控分布漂移下的生成器学习过程。

Result: 在涉及合成PDE系统和分布外傅里叶神经算子（FNO）监控的19个实验中，SEAM能够检测出即便局部预测准确但全局解释不兼容的情况，并将失败归因到具体通道和重叠区域。论文建立了最小成本干预和守恒合同可检测性定理，以及可辨识性和闭包可恢复性的配套结果。

Conclusion: SEAM为现有求解器和学习模型增加了全局解释一致性审计能力，检验其局部解释能否形成连贯的科学账户。该框架是生成器无关的，适用于多种科学机器学习场景。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning commonly validates models at the level of a subdomain, a benchmark split, or an explanation for one prediction. Yet such local checks cannot establish whether the resulting explanations can be assembled into one globally admissible explanation. We introduce Scientific Explanation-Admissibility Machines (SEAM), a generator-agnostic framework that makes this local-to-global consistency question computable across regions, sensors, regimes, and model components. The finite explanation-sheaf instantiation SEAM-$Ω$ represents each region by a structured explanation with state, closure, and observation channels together with optional contract metadata; compares neighboring explanations on their overlaps; and converts disagreement into a channel-resolved obstruction. This obstruction locates inconsistency and tests competing declared accounts by restricting each repair to the revisions that one account permits. Exact feasibility refutes or retains an account; when exact repair is unavailable, residual-aware regularized records provide a separately labeled empirical attribution. The framework also separates inconsistency from non-identifiability and monitors learned generators under distribution shift. We establish theorems for minimum-cost intervention and conservation-contract detectability, together with companion results for identifiability and closure recoverability. Across nineteen experiments involving synthetic partial differential equation systems and out-of-distribution Fourier neural operator (FNO) monitoring, SEAM detects incompatible explanations even when local predictions are accurate, and attributes failures to specific channels and overlaps. SEAM adds a global explanation-consistency audit to existing solvers and learning models, testing whether their local explanations form a coherent scientific account.

</details>


### [306] [Spectral Aliasing Pretext: A novel task for Self-Supervised fault diagnosis in rotating machinery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05705)
*Victor Gialis,Maxime Metz,David Esteve,Abdenour Soualhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出频谱混叠预文本（SAP）自监督学习方法，通过对振动信号进行欠采样创建折叠频谱，训练Transformer重构原始频谱，从而在无标签数据上学习故障诊断特征；在CWRU数据集上，SAP结合线性探测用少量标签数据即可达到高分类性能，且优于全监督训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在机械故障诊断中需要大量标注数据，而工业场景中标注数据稀缺，因此需要一种无需大量标签的自监督预训练方法。

Method: 提出频谱混叠预文本（SAP）：对振动信号进行故意欠采样，产生频谱混叠（折叠频谱），然后训练一个Transformer模型来重构原始未折叠的频谱，从而学习频域不变特征。

Result: 在CWRU数据集上，SAP预训练后使用线性探测（仅用少量标签数据）即可快速达到很高的分类性能，且方差低；而全微调（包括全监督训练）并未带来更稳定或更好的结果。

Conclusion: SAP结合线性探测在标签数据有限的情况下比全监督训练更有效、更可靠，为机械故障诊断提供了一种新的自监督预训练范式。

Abstract: Deep learning is a new way for machinery fault diagnosis but requires extensive labeled data, a scarce resource in industrial settings. We propose Spectral Aliasing Pretext (SAP), a self-supervised learning method that pretrains models on unlabeled vibration data by exploiting spectral aliasing. We deliberately undersample signals to create folded spectrum, then train a Transformer to reconstruct the original unfolded spectrum. This pretext task forces the model to learn frequency-domain invariants characteristic of mechanical faults, without potentially destructive augmentations. Experiments on the CWRU dataset show that SAP learns stable and highly discriminative representations. In a linear probing setting, SAP quickly achieves very high classification performance with only a small fraction of labeled data and low variance. In contrast, full fine-tuning, including fully supervised training, does not lead to more stable or better results. Overall, these findings suggest that SAP combined with linear probing can be more effective and reliable than fully supervised training for fault diagnosis with limited labeled data.

</details>


### [307] [CircuitSteer: Geometrically Aligned Multi-Layer Steering via Sparse Autoencoder Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05732)
*Mehrshad Saadatinia,Parsa Razmara,Ardalan Aryashad,Ali Abbasi,Seyedarmin Azizi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CircuitSteer是一个利用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）识别并操纵多层语义电路的框架，通过特征流电路构建和几何对齐的密集引导向量，实现比传统单层干预方法更鲁棒和有效的LLM行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM行为控制方法（如CAA）依赖固定的单层干预，无法在不同语义输入中保持一致的行为变化，且跨层干预效果有限，亟需一种更鲁棒的多层电路级控制方法。

Method: 1) 使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）识别分布在多层的语义特征；2) 基于特征共激活和编码器方向几何对齐构建特征流电路，隔离目标行为对应的多层子电路；3) 从稀疏特征中合成密集引导向量，实施多点干预来引导模型内部语义轨迹。

Result: 在毒性、情感强度、谄媚和拒绝等多种任务上，CircuitSteer是唯一在所有模型和数据集中一致保持流畅性的干预方法，而其他方法要么牺牲文本质量要么缺乏覆盖范围，在复杂行为（如谄媚和拒绝）上完全失败。

Conclusion: 通过强制选定特征间的几何对齐，多层电路引导相比静态单点干预能产生更鲁棒和有效的行为控制，证明了多层电路干预的优越性。

Abstract: Controlling the behavior of large language models (LLMs) remains a critical challenge for AI alignment. Existing steering methods, such as Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA), typically rely on fixed single-layer interventions derived from aggregate activation differences. These methods impose a single intervention across semantically diverse inputs and often fail to sustain consistent behavioral changes across layers, limiting the effectiveness of the steering. In this work, we introduce CircuitSteer, a novel framework that leverages Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to identify and manipulate coherent semantic circuits distributed across multiple layers. By constructing a feature flow circuit based on feature co-activation and the geometric alignment of decoder directions, we isolate the specific multi-layer subcircuits responsible for a target behavior. We then synthesize dense steering vectors from these sparse features and apply multi-point interventions to guide the model's internal semantic trajectory. We evaluate CircuitSteer using contrastive examples across a diverse set of tasks, including toxicity, emotion-intensity, sycophancy, and refusal, spanning two model families. Across all models and datasets, CircuitSteer is the only method to consistently produce fluency-preserving interventions; competing methods either sacrifice text quality or lack coverage, failing entirely on complex behaviors like sycophancy and refusal. These results demonstrate that multi-layer circuit steering, enabled by enforcing geometric alignment among selected features, yields strictly more robust and effective behavioral control than static single-point interventions. Code is available at https://github.com/mehrshad-sdtn/CircuitSteer.

</details>


### [308] [Multivariate Time Series Forecasting needs Cross Variable Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05742)
*Kuiye Ding,Yifan Hu,Hanchen Wang,Hao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多变量时间序列预测中未来变量之间依赖关系被忽视的问题，提出了一种名为CvLoss的跨变量损失函数，作为结构正则化器约束预测残差的跨变量图一致性，显著提升了多种预测模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型主要关注历史观测中的跨变量依赖关系，却很少探索未来值之间的依赖关系。主流的直接预测（DF）范式使用逐点目标生成多步预测，没有显式约束跨变量结构，导致在存在跨变量和滞后依赖时存在目标不匹配的问题。

Method: 提出CvLoss（Cross-Variable Loss），一种即插即用的结构正则化器，通过构建跨变量图约束预测残差。CvLoss惩罚预测块上不一致的边级残差差异，鼓励同步和异步交互的一致性。

Result: 实验表明CvLoss能够持续改进具有竞争力的预测模型，优于代表性的学习目标，并且与多种预测主干网络兼容。

Conclusion: 跨变量未来依赖关系的建模对多变量时间序列预测至关重要，CvLoss作为一种轻量级正则化方法，有效弥补了直接预测范式的目标偏差，值得在更广泛的预测模型中应用。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting presents unique challenges because future variables often co-evolve under shared system dynamics. While existing studies mainly focus on cross-variable dependencies in historical observations, dependencies among future values are much less explored. Specifically, modern forecasting models largely follow the Direct Forecasting (DF) paradigm, generating multi-step forecasts with point-wise objectives that do not explicitly constrain cross-variable structure. In this work, we show that the DF objective is mismatched in the presence of cross-variable and lagged dependencies, revealing an objective gap. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{C}ross-\textbf{V}ariable \textbf{Loss} (CvLoss), a plug-in structural regularizer that constrains forecast residuals on a cross-variable graph. CvLoss penalizes inconsistent edge-wise residual differences over forecast patches, encouraging consistency across both synchronous and asynchronous interactions. Our experiments show that CvLoss consistently improves competitive forecasting models, outperforms representative learning objectives, and is compatible with a variety of forecasting backbones.

</details>


### [309] [Equipment-centric workpiece localization in near real-time using deep learning-based vision and event-driven finite state machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05744)
*Dohyeon Kong,Jaebong Cho,Hyunbo Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于设备中心视角的框架，利用多个静态2D摄像头观测搬运设备，通过3D坐标估计、活动识别（关键点引导注意力机制的3D CNN）和事件驱动有限状态机，在高温、表面退化、路径不规则的热锻造环境中实现连续工件定位，工厂实测达到100%事件检测准确率（33秒容差窗），平均定位误差317.8mm，平均延迟21秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热锻造过程中工件连续定位对可追溯性和工艺协调至关重要，但直接跟踪因极端温度、表面退化和不规则路径而不可靠，需要一种不依赖工件自身标识的间接定位方法。

Method: 提出设备中心框架：1) 多静态2D摄像头观测搬运设备；2) 估计设备在工厂平面图中的3D坐标；3) 利用集成关键点引导注意力机制的3D CNN识别抓取和释放活动；4) 事件驱动有限状态机验证活动为离散搬运事件，并连续更新工件状态和位置。

Result: 在实际热锻造工厂评估：33秒容差窗内事件检测准确率100%，平均定位误差317.8mm，平均系统延迟21秒。支持工件转移可视化和设备操作定量分析。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将视觉感知与可解释的事件驱动推理相结合，在工业热锻造环境中实现了可靠、低延迟的连续工件定位，为生产线追溯和工艺协调提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Continuous workpiece localization is essential for traceability and process coordination in hot forging, but direct tracking is unreliable because of extreme temperatures, surface degradation, and irregular routing. This study presents an equipment-centric framework that infers workpiece locations from handling equipment observed by multiple static 2D cameras. The framework estimates floorplan-space 3D equipment coordinates and recognizes grasp and release activities. Event-driven finite state machines validate these activities as discrete handling events and continuously update workpiece states and locations. A keypoint-guided attention mechanism integrated into a 3D convolutional neural network improves activity recognition by focusing on functionally relevant equipment regions. Evaluation in an operational hot forging factory achieved 100\% event detection accuracy within a 33-second tolerance window, a mean localization error of 317.8 mm, and a mean system latency of 21 seconds. The framework connects vision-based perception with interpretable event-driven reasoning and supports visualization of workpiece transfers and quantitative analysis of equipment operations.

</details>


### [310] [Accelerating nanodrug development in continuous flow systems using informed prediction models based on low-cost surrogate nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05761)
*Kai Dahms,Eilien Heinrich,Jochen Schmid,Michael Bortz,Iryna Savych,Regina Bleul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出并验证了一种基于形状约束的预测建模方法，用于在微流控制备条件下准确预测脂质体和脂质纳米颗粒的尺寸与多分散性指数，从而减少纳米治疗药物开发中依赖经验优化的实验筛选成本和时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米治疗药物的开发因工艺参数微小变化对纳米颗粒性质（如尺寸和多分散性）高度敏感，导致需要大量经验性优化和迭代实验筛选，缺乏预测性数学框架，增加了开发成本和时间。

Method: 采用基于形状约束的预测建模方法，结合实验数据和专家知识，在可控微流控条件下系统制备不同脂质浓度、流速和水相/有机相混合比例的脂质体和脂质纳米颗粒，并利用最小经验数据验证模型在制药应用中的有效性。

Result: 形状约束模型能够准确预测纳米颗粒的尺寸和多分散性，显著减少了对广泛实验工作流的需求，为纳米药物制造提供了理性高效的工艺开发支持。

Conclusion: 形状约束预测建模框架可有效替代传统经验优化，促进纳米治疗药物制造过程的合理化和效率提升，具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: The development of nanotherapeutics often involves extensive empirical optimization due to the sensitivity of nanoparticle properties, such as size and polydispersity index (PDI), to minor changes in process parameters. Factors like formulation concentration, flow rates, and mixing ratios can significantly influence clinical efficacy and therapeutic outcomes. The absence of predictive mathematical frameworks has made iterative experimental screening necessary, increasing both costs and development time.
  This study introduces and validates a predictive modeling approach based on shape constraints, aiming to enhance the estimation of nanoparticle characteristics across various process conditions. Using controlled microfluidic methods, liposomes and lipid nanoparticles were systematically prepared under varying lipid concentrations, flow rates, and aqueous-to-organic mixing ratios.
  The shape-constrained model, informed by both experimental data and expert knowledge, was subsequently validated for a pharmaceutical application using minimal empirical data. Results reveal that shape-constrained modeling facilitates accurate prediction of nanoparticle size and dispersity, reducing the need for extensive experimental workflows. This framework supports rational and efficient process development for manufacturing nanomedicine systems.

</details>


### [311] [Neuro-Symbolic Closed-Loop Control of Laser Powder Bed Fusion with an In-Loop Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05773)
*Gisuk Hong,Jaebong Cho,Hyunbo Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种几何条件化的神经符号闭环架构用于激光粉末床熔融，其中符合标准的本体运行在控制回路内，将符号推理与统计学习相结合，设置约束感知预测控制器的目标。通过将不可测量的熔池深度映射到可观测的宽度（高斯过程建模深度-宽度比及其校准不确定性），并结合描述逻辑推理器分类特征并选择约束，在Eagar-Tsai代理模型上验证了该架构消除悬垂熔渣、零熔渣下仅少量熔合不足、对故意植物失配优雅降级，以及通过编辑本体数据而非代码重新定位到新合金的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光粉末床熔融过程中，悬垂熔渣等质量缺陷受熔池深度控制，但熔池深度无法在建造过程中直接测量，需要一种能够结合符号知识与数据驱动模型、实现闭环控制的方法。

Method: 提出一种几何条件化的神经符号闭环架构：包含一个符合标准的本体（将过程目标与约束映射到控制器可观测的信号），描述逻辑推理器将目标和约束转换为每个扫描的参考值和边界；通过高斯过程建立几何和功率相关的深度-宽度比及其校准不确定性，从而将不可测量的熔池深度约束映射到可观测的宽度约束；推理器分类即将到来的特征并选择激活约束（如在悬垂处添加熔合不足下限、在校准范围外添加单调保护、在声明工艺窗口时添加能量密度上限），仅在几何上下文变化时运行推理，否则在每扫描路径上只求解一个小二次规划问题。

Result: 在标定到NIST AM-Bench IN625基准的Eagar-Tsai代理模型上，该架构消除了几何盲控制器产生的熔渣；在双重评分下保持零熔渣且仅有少量残余熔合不足；在故意植物失配下优雅降级；可通过编辑本体数据而非代码重新定位到新合金和约束。

Conclusion: 结果建立了架构可行性，下一步主要工作是实验校准深度-宽度比。

Abstract: A geometry-conditioned, neuro-symbolic closed-loop architecture is proposed for laser powder bed fusion, in which a standards-aligned ontology operates inside the control loop and couples symbolic reasoning with statistical learning to set the targets of a constraint-aware predictive controller. The ontology links the process objectives and constraints to the signals a controller can observe, and a description-logic reasoner converts them into the references and bounds enforced on each scan. The demonstrated case is overhang dross, a quality limit on the melt pool depth, which governs quality yet cannot be measured during the build, is mapped through a geometry- and power-dependent depth-to-width ratio onto a bound on the observable width, with the ratio and its calibrated uncertainty supplied by a Gaussian process. The reasoner classifies each upcoming feature and selects the active constraints-adding a lack-of-fusion floor at overhangs, a monotone guard beyond the calibrated range, and an energy-density cap where a process window is declared while running only on changes of geometric context and otherwise leaving a single small quadratic program on the per-scan path. In an Eagar-Tsai surrogate calibrated to the NIST AM-Bench benchmark for IN625, the architecture eliminates the dross produced by a geometry-blind controller, holds dross at zero with only a small residual lack-of-fusion under dual scoring, degrades gracefully under deliberate plant mismatch, and retargets to new alloys and constraints by editing ontology data rather than code. The results establish architectural feasibility, experimental calibration of the ratio is the principal next step.

</details>


### [312] [GROM: Gradient-Free Rapid One-Shot Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05783)
*Paweł Batorski,Przemysław Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GROM，一种基于闭式解析解的一次性机器遗忘方法，通过岭回归最小二乘优化直接修改权重，无需迭代微调，在多个遗忘基准上达到最优的遗忘-效用权衡，并能抵抗低比特量化攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于微调的遗忘方法计算成本高、依赖迭代优化，且知识可能只是被隐藏而非移除（如量化可恢复）。需要一种高效、直接、可验证移除的遗忘方法。

Method: 将遗忘建模为岭正则化最小二乘优化问题，推导出闭式加性权重更新。该更新迫使选定层抑制目标内容，同时严格保持对保留数据的行为。仅需前向传播（无梯度、无反向传播），单次计算即可完成权重编辑。

Result: 在 TOFU-5%/10%、MUSE-Books/News、WMDP 上取得最优遗忘-效用权衡；计算开销比微调低数个数量级；能抵御低比特量化攻击（恢复遗忘知识）。

Conclusion: GROM 提供了一种快速、可验证的一次性遗忘方案，从根本上移除权重中的目标知识，而非隐藏，兼具高效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Machine unlearning has become a critical capability for safely removing specific, sensitive knowledge from large language models (LLMs). Current state-of-the-art approaches primarily rely on iterative, training-time unlearning via fine-tuning. However, even when utilizing parameter-efficient dimensionality reduction techniques like LoRA, gradient-based optimization remains computationally expensive and lacks explicit analytical formulations. It can also leave the targeted knowledge merely hidden rather than removed, to the point that simply quantizing the unlearned model restores much of what it was supposed to have erased. To resolve this, we propose a novel one-shot unlearning approach, abandoning iterative optimization in favor of a direct, exact analytical solution. We frame the unlearning process as a ridge-regularized least-squares optimization problem, deriving a closed-form additive update for targeted weight matrices. This update forces the selected layer to suppress unwanted content while strictly preserving its behavior on retained data. Computed from gradient-free forward passes alone, with no backpropagation and no iteration to convergence, GROM applies the weight edit in mere seconds, which makes it orders of magnitude faster than traditional fine-tuning. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that GROM achieves state-of-the-art forgetting-utility trade-offs on TOFU-5%, TOFU-10%, MUSE-Books, MUSE-News and WMDP, significantly reducing computational overhead without sacrificing overall model performance. Because the update removes the targeted content from the weights instead of masking it, GROM also withstands the low-bit quantization attack that recovers much of the content a gradient-based baseline had appeared to forget. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Batorskq/GROM.

</details>


### [313] [Predicting Task Difficulty Without Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05797)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无需 rollout 的 ex ante 任务难度预测，在 17 个 agentic 基准（编码、数学、机器学习、网页导航、函数调用等）上验证 token 级熵作为预测信号，发现 AUC 可能掩盖差的估计，且实际与预期难度的残差可暴露环境缺陷（如污染、不可行性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务难度预估能帮助设计者校准评估基准、构建渐进式训练课程，尤其在长周期任务中可避免昂贵的试错模拟。先前方法局限于静态任务或孤立编码环境，依赖狭窄特征且评估指标不精确。

Method: 跨 17 种 agentic benchmarks 进行 ex ante 难度预测研究，涵盖多种领域。分析 AUC 的局限性，识别 token 级熵作为有效预测信号，并通过期望难度与实际难度的残差分析来揭示环境缺陷。

Result: AUC 可能掩盖难度估计的不足；token 级熵是有效的预测特征；实际与预期难度的残差能暴露隐藏的环境问题（如污染和不可行性）。

Conclusion: 通过大规模跨基准实验，论文提供了可靠的 ex ante 难度预测方法，揭示了 AUC 等常用指标的缺陷，并展示了残差分析作为环境校准工具的价值。

Abstract: Task difficulty dictates an agent's likelihood of success, and estimating it without rollouts means forecasting this directly from a task description before executing costly simulations in stateful environments. Reliable estimates would therefore allow environment designers to calibrate evaluation benchmarks and construct progressive training curricula. This becomes increasingly important as agents move into long-horizon domains, where empirical trial-and-error is a severe computational bottleneck. Prior work on early prediction is limited to static tasks or isolated coding environments, often relying on narrow features and inaccurate evaluation metrics. We study \textit{ex ante} difficulty prediction across 17 agentic benchmarks spanning coding, mathematics, machine learning, web navigation, function calling, and other domains. We show that AUC can mask poor difficulty estimates, identify token-level entropy as a useful predictive signal, and show how residuals between expected and observed difficulty can expose hidden environment flaws such as contamination and infeasibility.

</details>


### [314] [Learning to Rank Tensor Network Contraction Plans for GPU-Accelerated Quantum Circuit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05819)
*Alfred M. Pastor,Maribel Castillo,Jose M. Badia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于学习排序（Learning to Rank）的框架，用于在GPU上高效选择张量网络收缩方案，避免执行高代价的收缩计划。通过从收缩计划的逐对操作序列中提取结构特征，训练梯度提升排序器（listwise和pairwise目标），在多种电路族上评估，发现listwise模型决策质量最佳，且模型在不同GPU架构间保持一定的排序稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟在量子算法开发中至关重要，但电路规模增大导致计算成本急剧上升。张量网络收缩能利用电路结构降低复杂度，但收缩方案的效率高度依赖具体计划。在GPU上，理论复杂度相似的方案因并行性、归约结构、内存流量和收缩几何等因素表现迥异。因此需要一种高效的方法来预选最优收缩计划，避免盲目执行或穷举搜索。

Method: 采用学习排序（Learning to Rank）框架：每个收缩计划由其逐对收缩序列的结构特征表示；使用梯度提升树（gradient-boosted rankers）作为排序模型，分别以listwise和pairwise损失函数进行训练；训练数据来自真实GPU测量结果。评估时使用分布内测试集和电路族迁移测试集，并与随机选择和MinFill基线对比。还研究了后端迁移性，比较两种GPU架构上的经验排序，并评估源设备训练模型在目标设备上的表现。

Result: 学习排序模型普遍优于随机和MinFill基线，其中listwise模型提供最强的整体决策质量。在不同GPU架构间，排序结果基本（但不完全）稳定，源训练模型在目标设备上仍保有有用的决策质量。

Conclusion: 学习排序是一种实用的收缩方案搜索缩减手段，能够在不执行完整计算的情况下预选高效计划，从而加速经典模拟。但性能仍部分依赖于后端设备，提示未来工作需考虑跨架构适应性。

Abstract: Classical simulation remains essential for developing and validating quantum algorithms, but its cost grows rapidly with circuit size. Tensor-network contraction can reduce this cost by exploiting circuit structure, although its efficiency depends strongly on the chosen contraction plan. On GPUs, plans with similar theoretical complexity may perform very differently because execution also depends on parallelism, reduction structure, memory traffic, and contraction geometry. We present a learning-to-rank framework for selecting efficient contraction plans before executing them. Each plan is represented by structural features derived directly from its sequence of pairwise contractions, and gradient-boosted rankers are trained from GPU measurements using listwise and pairwise objectives. We evaluate the resulting models on diverse circuit families, using separate in-distribution and circuit-family-shift test sets, and compare them with random and MinFill-based baselines. The learned rankers generally identify better plans, with the listwise model providing the strongest overall decision quality. We also study backend shift by comparing empirical plan orderings on two GPU architectures and evaluating the source-trained models on the second device without retraining. The rankings remain substantially, though not perfectly, stable across GPUs, and the models retain useful decision quality. These results support Learning to Rank as a practical way to reduce contraction-plan search, while showing that performance remains partly backend dependent.

</details>


### [315] [Evidential Rule Learning for Interpretable Classification with Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05859)
*Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FERL (Fast Evidential Rule Learning) 是一种可解释的模糊规则学习方法，其输出具有证据性质（信念、似然、弃权），通过单次确定性前向传递直接从模糊隶属度中获得这些能力，无需后处理校准、辅助头、留出集或重复推理。理论分析证明FERL是Lipschitz稳定的，确保证据输出随输入平滑变化。在30个表格数据集基准上，FERL的准确率显著优于最先进的规则学习器（比第二名平均高+2.6%）；其集合预测在可信分类器中实现了最佳效用折扣准确率（u65/u80=0.80/0.83 vs 0.79/0.80），且集合覆盖率更高（0.92 vs ≤0.82）；在表格数据近端OOD检测上匹配专用检测器（AUROC 77.7 vs 77.4）；在CUB和AwA2的概念瓶颈评估中，距离最强专用检测器仅差2.3 AUROC点，同时取得最佳AwA2 AUPR-Out（68.3）和新类拒绝性能（57.2），并能指出哪些属性异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释分类在实际部署中不仅需要准确预测，还需要对决策背后的证据保持透明，并在无法可靠决策时能够弃权。现有方法要么缺乏内在的证据量化能力，要么依赖后处理校准、辅助头、留出集或重复推理来获得弃权或不确定度估计，这增加了复杂性且可能损害可解释性。因此，需要一种能直接从模型结构中产生证据性输出（信念、似然、弃权）的可解释规则学习方法。

Method: FERL通过学习模糊规则模型，其输出（信念、似然、弃权）直接来自模糊隶属度的单次确定性前向传递，无需辅助头、留出集或重复推理。理论分析证明FERL满足Lipschitz稳定性，即证据输出随输入平滑变化，不会出现剧烈波动。

Result: 在30个表格数据集基准上，FERL准确率显著优于最先进规则学习器（平均比第二名高+2.6%）；其集合预测在可信分类器中达到最佳效用折扣准确率（u65/u80=0.80/0.83 vs 0.79/0.80），集合覆盖率更高（0.92 vs ≤0.82）；表格数据近端OOD检测AUROC达77.7，匹配专用检测器（77.4）；在CUB和AwA2概念瓶颈评估中距最强专用检测器仅差2.3 AUROC点，取得最佳AwA2 AUPR-Out（68.3）和新类拒绝性能（57.2），并能指出哪些属性异常。

Conclusion: FERL提供了一种兼具可解释性、准确性和证据能力的规则学习方法，具有理论稳定性保证，在分类、OOD检测和概念瓶颈评估等多个任务上均表现优异，验证了其在实际部署中的潜力。

Abstract: Interpretable classification often requires more than accurate predictions for real-life deployment: models should be transparent about the evidence behind their decisions and abstain when they cannot decide reliably. We introduce Fast Evidential Rule Learning (FERL), a method that learns interpretable, accurate fuzzy rule models whose outputs are evidential. Unlike post-hoc calibration, FERL's belief, plausibility, and abstention capabilities arise directly from the fuzzy memberships in a single deterministic pass, with no auxiliary head, held-out set, or repeated inference. Our theoretical analysis further shows that FERL is Lipschitz stable, which means that its evidential outputs vary smoothly with the input. Against state-of-the-art rule learners, FERL is statistically significantly more accurate across a 30 tabular-dataset benchmark ($+2.6\%$ average accuracy over the second best). Its native set predictions attain the best utility-discounted accuracy among credal classifiers ($u_{65}/u_{80}=0.80/0.83$ vs.\ $0.79/0.80$ for the naive credal classifier), at higher set coverage ($0.92$ vs.\ $\le0.82$). FERL also matches dedicated out-of-distribution detectors on tabular near-OOD detection ($77.7$ vs.\ $77.4$ AUROC for the strongest baseline). Under detector-class-disjoint concept-bottleneck evaluation, its it is within $2.3$ AUROC points of the strongest dedicated detector on both CUB and AwA2, while attaining the best AwA2 AUPR-Out ($68.3$) and novel-class rejection ($57.2$), while being able to name which attributes are anomalous.

</details>


### [316] [Beyond Feature Importance: A Comparative Analysis of Pattern Detection Methods in Cluster Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05880)
*Benjamin Connor,Anna Jurek-Loughrey,Lu Bai,Muhammad Fahim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对聚类结果进行模式检测的后验分析方法的比较评估。通过引入具有预定义模式注入的合成数据集，评估了三种常用技术：随机森林代理模型（结合置换特征重要性）、LIME和主成分分析。结果表明，尽管每种方法都能成功恢复相关特征，但没有任何一种方法能一致地检测所有注入的模式类型，这揭示了现有可解释性工具与聚类模式级解释需求之间的差距，从而需要专用的模式检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类结果解释面临挑战，尤其在医疗等领域需要从高维数据中提取有意义模式。现有可解释性技术主要针对特征重要性或局部实例级解释，而非识别聚类中存在的结构化模式。因此需要评估常用后验分析方法在聚类模式检测中的效果。

Method: 使用具有预定义注入模式的合成数据集进行受控评估。比较随机森林代理模型（含置换特征重要性）、LIME和主成分分析三种技术，检测它们在不同模式类型上的表现。

Result: 每种方法都能成功恢复相关特征，但没有一种方法能一致检测所有注入的模式类型。不同方法在不同模式类型上有差异。

Conclusion: 现有可解释性工具与聚类模式级解释需求之间存在关键差距，需要开发专用的模式检测方法。

Abstract: Interpreting clustering outcomes remains a fundamental challenge in data analysis, particularly in domains such as healthcare where meaningful patterns must be extracted from high-dimensional data. While numerous explainability techniques exist, they are primarily designed to assess feature importance or provide local instance-level explanations rather than to identify structured patterns present within clusters. This work presents a comparative evaluation of commonly used post-hoc analysis methods for pattern detection in clustering results. To enable controlled evaluation, we introduce a suite of synthetic datasets in which predefined patterns are systematically injected. Three widely used techniques are evaluated: a Random Forest surrogate model with permutation feature importance, LIME (Local Interpretable Model-agnostic Explanations), and principal component analysis. Results demonstrate that although each method can successfully recover relevant features, none consistently detects all injected pattern types. These findings high- light a critical gap between existing explainability tools and the requirements of pattern-level cluster interpretation, motivating the development of dedicated pattern detection methodologies.

</details>


### [317] [Alternating Levenberg-Marquardt Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks with Fourier-Enhanced Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05892)
*Yulun Wu,Matthieu Barreau,Miguel Aguiar,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种傅里叶增强的交替Levenberg-Marquardt PINN（FALM-PINN）优化框架，通过解耦表示学习与系数拟合，有效解决PINN在高频、多尺度及强非线性PDE上的精度不足问题，相对误差比现有最优方法降低最多两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINN在求解高频/多尺度解及强非线性PDE时表现不佳，根本原因在于频谱偏差（神经网络倾向于低估高频特征）和表示-系数耦合（表示学习与系数拟合纠缠在同一非凸优化目标中）。现有方法未能同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出FALM-PINN框架，采用双层交替训练：上层问题学习一种傅里叶增强的基函数，以丰富潜空间的高频成分；下层问题通过Levenberg-Marquardt算法求解非线性最小二乘问题，拟合该基上的投影系数，从而解耦表示学习与系数拟合。该框架适用于一般非线性及耦合PDE系统，对线性PDE可简化为单步凸优化问题，并证明了交替训练方案的全局收敛性。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的高频和非线性PDE数值算例中，FALM-PINN取得的相对L²误差比现有最优基线方法低最多两个数量级。

Conclusion: FALM-PINN通过解耦与傅里叶增强机制，同时克服了频谱偏差和表示-系数耦合两大障碍，显著提升了PINN对高频及非线性PDE的求解精度与收敛性，为物理信息神经网络的发展提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often fail to accurately resolve partial differential equations (PDEs) with high-frequency or multi-scale solutions, as well as strongly nonlinear problems. Two factors underlie this difficulty: spectral bias, the tendency of neural networks to underfit high-frequency features; and representation-coefficient coupling, the entanglement of representation learning and coefficient fitting within a single nonconvex optimization objective. In this work, we propose the Fourier-enhanced alternating Levenberg--Marquardt PINN (FALM-PINN), an optimization framework that decouples representation learning from coefficient fitting. The upper-level problem learns a Fourier-enhanced basis that enriches the latent space with high-frequency components, while the lower-level problem resolves the coupling by fitting the projection coefficients on this basis, solving a nonlinear least-squares problem with the Levenberg--Marquardt algorithm. The framework applies to general nonlinear and coupled PDE systems, and reduces to a single-step convex optimization problem for linear PDEs. We prove global convergence of the alternating training scheme in both cases. Numerical examples on multiple challenging high-frequency and nonlinear PDEs show that FALM-PINN achieves relative $L^2$ errors up to two orders of magnitude lower than state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [318] [CohortHijack: Robustness of Single Cell Annotation to Companion Cell Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05900)
*Arash Vashagh,Yasmin Vashagh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出 CohortHijack 方法，通过移除查询队列中的非目标细胞来操纵单细胞注释中的标签细化步骤，而不改变目标细胞自身表达谱或基础预测。实验表明，结构化移除和多种搜索策略能有效改变细化标签，揭示了查询队列组成作为攻击面的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单细胞注释工具利用邻近细胞或聚类投票来细化初始标签，但该细化过程可能被恶意操控。研究者希望探究是否可以在不改变目标细胞本身的情况下，仅通过修改查询队列中的其他细胞来操纵最终的细化标签。

Method: 提出 CohortHijack 审计方法，通过移除查询队列中的非目标细胞（保持目标细胞表达谱、基础预测和训练模型不变）来测试细化鲁棒性。在 PBMC3K 和 Paul15 数据集上，使用逻辑回归和校准线性 SVM 分类器，评估随机移除、结构化移除以及贪婪搜索、多起点搜索、束搜索等策略。同时测试了 CellTypist 的多数投票机制。

Result: 结构化移除在 Paul15 上效果优于随机移除。多起点搜索分别改变了 24.33%（线性 SVM）和 19.67%（逻辑回归）的目标标签，同时仅移除少量队列细胞并保持平均附带变化低于 0.4%。消融实验证实当禁用邻域细化时该效应消失。CellTypist 的独立预测未受影响，但细化标签在移除少量伴细胞后发生变化。

Conclusion: 查询队列组成是单细胞注释中一个可被利用的攻击面，即使保留目标细胞表达和基础预测不变，也能通过调整队列细胞来操纵细化标签。这提示现有注释工具的鲁棒性存在隐患，需要加强防御。

Abstract: Many single-cell annotation tools refine an initial cell label using nearby cells or cluster-level voting. We study whether this refinement can be manipulated without changing the target cell. We introduce CohortHijack, a robustness audit that removes selected non-target cells from the query cohort while preserving the target expression profile, base prediction, and trained model. We evaluate random and structured removal methods, together with greedy, multi-start, and beam search, on PBMC3K and Paul15 using logistic regression and calibrated linear SVM classifiers. Structured removal was consistently stronger than random removal on Paul15. Multi-start search changed 24.33% of linear-SVM targets and 19.67% of logistic-regression targets while removing a small fraction of the cohort and keeping mean collateral changes below 0.4%. Ablations confirmed that the effect disappeared when neighborhood refinement was disabled. We also evaluated CellTypist majority voting, where independent predictions remained unchanged across all evaluations, but refined labels changed after small companion-cell removals. These findings identify query cohort composition as a target-preserving attack surface in single-cell annotation.

</details>


### [319] [BioM-JEPA: joint-embedding prediction of graph-connected gene blocks in single cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05928)
*Yuhao Wang,Zelin Zang,Yuxuan Liu,Zhen Lei,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioM-JEPA 是一种联合嵌入预测架构，通过预测蛋白质关联和共表达证据定义的图连接基因块的聚合表示，在单细胞转录组学中实现了比传统方法更优的表示学习效果，同时显著提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞转录组数据稀疏且存在大量缺失观测，但现有自监督模型大多基于单个基因的重构任务进行学习，未能充分利用基因间协调的生物学程序信息。需要一种能够捕获基因模块化协同表达模式的表示学习方法。

Method: BioM-JEPA 采用联合嵌入预测架构，定义基于蛋白质关联和语料库共表达证据的图连接基因块；学生网络从细胞中剩余基因推断每个目标块的表示，缓慢更新的教师网络从完整基因集提供对应目标；使用线性注意力避免二次复杂度的基因-基因注意力矩阵。

Result: 块级预测相比 token 预测、随机块和重构控制产生更高有效秩的嵌入且与检测基因深度的关联更弱；在 CellBench 任务中，冻结的 BioM-JEPA 嵌入保留了表达、通路和邻域信息，并取得最低的聚合扰动响应误差；在 hPancreas 实验中，微调吞吐量比 scFoundation 高 5.75 倍，保留嵌入吞吐量高 3.76 倍。

Conclusion: 图连接基因块是单细胞生物学中 JEPA 风格表示学习的有效预测单元，BioM-JEPA 在生物学保真度和计算效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Single-cell transcriptomes are sparse observations of coordinated biological programmes, yet most self-supervised models learn by reconstructing individual genes. Here we present BioM-JEPA, a joint-embedding predictive architecture that instead predicts aggregate representations of graph-connected gene blocks defined by protein-association and corpus-derived coexpression evidence. A student network infers each target-block representation from the remaining genes in a cell, while a slowly updated teacher supplies the corresponding target from the full observed gene set. Under the reported extraction procedure, block-level prediction produced embeddings with higher effective rank and weaker association with detected-gene depth in the tested diagnostics than token-prediction, random-block and reconstruction controls. Across CellBench tasks, frozen BioM-JEPA embeddings retained expression, pathway and neighbourhood information and achieved the lowest aggregate perturbation-response error among the evaluated models. Representation diagnostics were also consistent with canonical pancreatic programmes and compositional relationships between genetic perturbations. Linear attention avoids constructing a quadratic gene-by-gene attention matrix; in a matched one-epoch hPancreas experiment at batch size 8, BioM-JEPA provided 5.75-fold higher fine-tuning throughput and 3.76-fold higher held-out embedding throughput than scFoundation. Together, these results support graph-connected gene blocks as useful prediction units for JEPA-style representation learning in single-cell biology.

</details>


### [320] [How Far Do Simple Transformations Translate Across Text Embedding Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05980)
*Sid Ali Hamideche,Louis Adrien Dufrene,Quentin Lampin,Guillaume Larue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探究了简单线性变换能否在不同文本嵌入模型之间转换表征，发现虽然对于一些兼容的模型对可以恢复共享结构并支持下游任务迁移，但整体上嵌入空间并不能通过简单的线性映射实现普遍转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解独立训练的嵌入模型如何组织语义信息对于实现AI间潜在通信（无需解码为人类可读文本）至关重要。先前文献存在“潜在通用性”假说，但缺乏在真实文本任务中的严格检验。

Method: 使用线性映射等轻量级翻译器，对9种在架构、池化策略和训练目标上各异的嵌入模型进行两两对齐。通过CKA（ centered kernel alignment）、下游迁移性能、忠实度（fidelity）和检索任务来评估跨模型表征的兼容性。

Result: 简单翻译器能恢复部分共享结构并支持某些兼容模型对之间的迁移，但在其他模型对上表现较差。兼容性取决于架构、训练目标、池化策略和数据分布的联合影响。

Conclusion: 异构嵌入空间之间并不存在文献中常假设的那种通过简单映射即可实现的普遍关系，即潜在通用性假说在真实文本设定中不成立。

Abstract: We investigate whether simple transformations can translate representations across heterogeneous text embedding models. Understanding how independently trained models organize semantic information is an enabler for AI-to-AI latent communication without decoding into human-readable text. Focusing on lightweight translators such as linear mappings, we test the literature hypothesis of latent universality in a realistic text setting beyond simplified benchmarks. Across nine embedding models differing in architecture, pooling strategy, and training objective, we evaluate compatibility using CKA, downstream transfer, fidelity, and retrieval. Simple translators recover meaningful shared structure and support transfer for some compatible pairs, but fail sharply for others. Compatibility depends jointly on architecture, training objective, pooling, and data distribution. Overall, the results show that heterogeneous embedding spaces are not universally related by simple mappings as often suggested in some literature.

</details>


### [321] [THBKG: A Temporal Biomedical Knowledge Graph for Decision-Aligned Clinical Advancement Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05982)
*Pui Chung Siu,Claudia Cabrera,Mani Mudaliar,Arkaitz Zubiaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个时间异构生物医学知识图谱（THBKG），包含110,396个实体和1,110万条边，每条边带有时间戳，能够回溯特定时间点的证据概况；基于此图构建了决策对齐的基准测试，预测靶点-疾病对从II期进入III期的成功率，实验表明图传播方法在缺乏直接证据的情况下仍能有效预测，并利用路径解释器提供可解释的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大约40-50%的II期临床疗效失败是由于靶点-疾病关联不足导致，因此需要一种方法在临床前阶段评估哪些项目最有可能成功。现有生物医学知识图谱无法按历史时间点组装证据概况，因此需要构建一个时间感知的知识图谱来支持回顾性验证和决策对齐的预测。

Method: 构建THBKG：包含110,396个实体（靶点、疾病、药物等）和11,106,000条边，涵盖19种关系类型，每条边记录其证据发生变化的时间年份，从而可以恢复任意过去时间点的证据概况。在此基础上定义决策对齐的基准：预测一个靶点-疾病对在进入II期时，基于可追溯至该决策点之前的证据，能否推进到III期。使用图传播方法（如图神经网络或路径传播）在THBKG上进行预测，并与仅使用直接证据的模型对比。同时采用基于路径的解释器，从决策时间子图中分解预测结果，提供可解释的证据景观。

Result: 图传播方法在THBKG上的预测表现优于所有直接证据参考模型（在治疗领域前十对中相对成功率达到4.3-4.5倍）。性能提升主要集中在72.8%的决策点无直接靶点-疾病证据的配对中，此时直接边模型无法做出预测，而编码器仍能实现5-6倍于随机水平的排序。路径解释器能够将每个预测分解为假设背后的证据景观。

Conclusion: THBKG能够作为持续更新的底物，用于通过回顾性验证研究治疗靶点假设。时间感知的知识图谱结合决策对齐的基准，可以更可靠地评估靶点-疾病关联的临床潜力，特别是对于缺乏直接证据的情况，并且路径解释增强了可解释性。

Abstract: Inadequate target--disease linkage accounts for 40--50\% of Phase~II efficacy failures, so anticipating which programmes will advance would let sponsors back the hypotheses most likely to reach patients. What a programme can be judged on is the evidence that supported its linkage \emph{when it entered the clinic}. No existing biomedical knowledge graph allows that evidence profile to be assembled as of a past date. We present the Temporal Heterogeneous Biomedical Knowledge Graph (THBKG), which describes and predicts therapeutic target--disease links through time: 110,396 entities and 11.1M edges across nineteen relation types, each edge carrying the year its evidence changed, so a pair's profile can be recovered as it stood when its own decision fell due. On this graph we define a decision-aligned benchmark that predicts, for a target--disease pair entering Phase~II, whether it advances to Phase~III on evidence datable before that decision. Graph propagation over the THBKG outranks every direct-evidence reference scored under the same decision-aligned protocol, reaching a relative success of 4.3--4.5 at the top ten pairs per therapeutic area. The gain concentrates on the 72.8\% of pairs with no direct target--disease evidence at their decision point, where a direct-edge model has nothing to read: the encoders still rank five- to sixfold above chance, recovering the signal by propagating over the intervening biology. Adapting a path-based explainer to the decision-time subgraph decomposes each prediction into the evidence landscape behind the hypothesis for explainable prediction. We release the THBKG as a continually updated substrate for studying therapeutic target hypotheses by retrospective validation.

</details>


### [322] [Observation-Grounded Self-Predictive Reinforcement Learning for Visual Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05989)
*Xinwei Liu,Junyuan Liang,Jianting Zhang,Wuhui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OG-SPR算法，通过结合多步潜在自预测和下一观测预测两个辅助目标，并引入轻量级适配器避免共享表示过度约束，在DeepMind Control Suite的28个视觉控制任务上提升了样本效率，尤其在高难度任务（如狗、人形机器人）上表现显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于像素的强化学习在样本效率上面临挑战。现有的动力学表示学习方法（潜在空间自预测或观测空间预测）在数据有限时仍难以处理复杂的视觉控制任务。仅依赖单一预测目标可能不足：观测预测将表示锚定在观测级动力学上，但未直接规范潜在表示在长时间跨度上的时间可预测性；而自预测则缺乏观测级约束。

Method: 提出OG-SPR（观测锚定的自预测表示），一种无模型的视觉RL算法。它同时使用多步潜在自预测和下一观测预测作为辅助目标。为避免共享表示被过度约束，引入两个轻量级适配器用于潜在自预测，使共享表示既能受益于时间预测信号，又不强制直接满足自预测目标。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite的28个视觉控制任务上，OG-SPR相比最先进的自预测和观测预测RL方法在整体性能上均有提升，在狗、人形机器人等高难度领域增益尤为明显。

Conclusion: 结合观测级动力学和潜在空间时间预测性，并引入适配器避免过度约束，能够有效提升视觉RL在有限数据下的样本效率和性能。

Abstract: Sample-efficient policy learning from pixels is a long-standing challenge in reinforcement learning (RL). Recent dynamics-based representation learning methods have significantly improved the sample efficiency of model-free visual RL by learning dynamics-aware representations through auxiliary prediction performed either in latent space (self-prediction) or observation space (observation prediction). However, state-of-the-art methods from both categories still struggle on challenging visual control tasks when training data is limited. We posit that relying on either predictive objective alone may be insufficient. In contrast, observation prediction grounds learned representations in observation-level dynamics, but does not directly regularize the temporal predictability of latent representations over extended horizons. In this paper, we propose Observation-Grounded Self-Predictive Representations (OG-SPR), a model-free visual RL algorithm for continuous control that learns representations that are both temporally predictive in latent space and grounded in observation-level dynamics. OG-SPR incorporates two core auxiliary objectives: multi-step latent self-prediction and next-observation prediction. We empirically show that directly imposing latent self-prediction on the shared representation may over-constrain it and does not necessarily improve performance. To address this issue, OG-SPR introduces two lightweight adapters for latent self-prediction, allowing the shared representation to benefit from temporally predictive signals without being forced to directly satisfy the self-prediction objective. Experiments on 28 visual control tasks from the DeepMind Control Suite show that OG-SPR improves aggregate performance over state-of-the-art self-predictive and observation-predictive RL methods, with particularly pronounced gains in challenging domains such as dog and humanoid.

</details>


### [323] [A Unified Risk View of Uncertainty: Posterior Risk for Disentanglement and Evaluation Beyond Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05995)
*Frieder Wizgall,Georg Tirpitz,Moritz Seiler,Kerstin Ritter,Bálint Mucsányi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了不确定性作为逐点后验风险（给定数据下，预测器在可能的真实函数分布下的期望损失）的统一定义，并基于此构建了一个理论支持的基准测试，利用半合成数据集直接计算或然不确定性和认知不确定性的真实值，从而避免代理评估，实现对不确定性估计的细粒度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键应用需要可靠的不确定性估计，这通常需要区分认知不确定性和偶然不确定性，但文献中这些概念的定义不一致，且真实认知不确定性通常无法获得，现有基准主要依赖代理任务（如分布外检测），无法提供完整的不确定性真值，限制了对不确定性估计结构和质量的洞察。

Method: 提出不确定性统一定义为逐点后验风险（给定数据下预测器在可能的真实函数分布下的期望损失），该定义结合了函数上的贝叶斯不确定性和与后验均值偏离的估计器依赖项（涵盖设定错误和优化误差）。基于该定义构建理论支持的基准测试，使用具有真实协变量和已知生成过程的半合成数据集，直接计算或然不确定性和认知不确定性的真值。

Result: 实验发现准确的预测并不能保证可靠的不确定性解耦；基准测试揭示了方法间实际有用的差异，识别出与真实不确定性目标有意义的对齐方法，同时暴露出对数据集和建模选择的敏感性。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一定义和基准测试为评估不确定性估计提供了理论支持，避免了代理评估的局限性，能够进行细粒度分析，并揭示了不同方法在不确定性解耦方面的实际差异。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimates are critical in safety-sensitive applications, where understanding the sources of predictive uncertainty is essential. This often requires disentangling epistemic uncertainty from aleatoric uncertainty, yet these uncertainty types are not defined consistently across the literature, making it difficult to assess whether a method produces accurate uncertainty estimates. Evaluation is further complicated by the fact that ground-truth epistemic uncertainty is typically unavailable. Existing benchmarks therefore mostly rely on proxy tasks such as out-of-distribution detection, which do not provide complete ground-truth uncertainty targets and offer limited insight into the structure and quality of uncertainty estimates. We propose a unified definition of uncertainty as pointwise posterior risk, the expected loss of a predictor under the distribution of plausible ground-truth functions given the data. This view combines Bayesian uncertainty over functions with estimator-dependent deviations from the posterior mean, capturing effects such as misspecification and optimization error. This formulation constitutes the foundation of a theory-backed benchmark that enables direct computation of oracle epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty using semi-synthetic datasets with real covariates and known generative processes. By avoiding proxy evaluations, the benchmark enables fine-grained analysis of uncertainty estimates. Empirically, we find that accurate prediction does not guarantee reliable uncertainty disentanglement. The benchmark reveals practically useful differences between methods, identifying approaches with meaningful alignment to oracle uncertainty targets while exposing sensitivity to datasets and modeling choices.

</details>


### [324] [Do Tabular Foundation Models Agree with Themselves?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06004)
*Christian Klötergens,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Tom Hanika*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs)作为表格预测任务的最先进方法，虽然通过自回归方式将单变量预测器转换为多变量预测器，但其生成的联合分布存在根本性问题：所有被评估的TFM在分类和回归任务中都违反了边际化一致性（marginalization consistency）和分解一致性（factorization consistency）两个关键要求，表明这些模型的预测不可能源自任何真实的联合分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TFMs是目前表格预测问题的最佳方法，但它们通过自回归采样将单变量预测器转换为多变量预测器，其联合分布的 faithfulness 尚未被研究。同时，由于真实联合分布未知，无法直接在真实数据集上评估TFM与后验分布的一致性。因此作者提出一个新的问题：模型的预测结果是否可能源自任何联合分布？

Method: 作者提出了两个任何有效的联合预测模型必须满足的一致性要求：(1) 边际化一致性（marginalization consistency）：边际化后的条件分布应等于直接预测的边际分布；(2) 分解一致性（factorization consistency）：不同的分解顺序应得到相同的联合分布。作者在多个数据集上对TFM进行这两项一致性检验。

Result: 所有被评估的TFM（无论是分类还是回归任务，在所有数据集上）都同时违反了边际化一致性和分解一致性要求。
